的梯度）有解析式， 所以正向加噪和反向采样都是逐步算出来的，没有神经网络也没有预录动画。 图像那栏只演示正向加噪与训练目标，因为真实图像的得分算不出来， 必须靠训练好的网络——本页没有这样的网络，也不会编造它的输出。"]}]]}]}]]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"vml-stage 95:T2dc4,<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line" data-line="1"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> numpy </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="2"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="3"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">T </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1000</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="4"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 线性调度：beta 从 1e-4 线性升到 0.02（原始 DDPM 的取值）</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="5"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">betas </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.linspace(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1e-4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0.02</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, T)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="6"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">alphas </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> betas</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="7"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">alpha_bars </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.cumprod(alphas)          </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># alpha_bar_t = prod_{s&#x3C;=t} alpha_s</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="8"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 注意下标：这里 t 从 0 数到 T-1，所以 alpha_bars[0] = 1 - beta_1 而不是 1。</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="9"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 课文里的公式把 t=0 留给「没加噪」，两种写法只差一格，别对错位。</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="10"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="11" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> forward_sample</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(x0, t, eps):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="12" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """x_t = sqrt(alpha_bar_t) * x0 + sqrt(1 - alpha_bar_t) * eps"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="13"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    ab </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> alpha_bars[t]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="14" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.sqrt(ab) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> x0 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.sqrt(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ab) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> eps</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="15"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="16"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">rng </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.random.default_rng(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="17"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">x0 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> rng.normal(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))           </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 假装这是一张 20x20 的图</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="18"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">eps </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> rng.normal(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">x0.shape)          </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 训练时的答案就是它</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="19"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="20"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> t </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">500</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">999</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="21"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    xt </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> forward_sample(x0, t, eps)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="22"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    ab </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> alpha_bars[t]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="23"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">    print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"t=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">t</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:4d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  alpha_bar=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">ab</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.4e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">  信号系数=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">np.sqrt(ab)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.4f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D">    # 如果去噪器完美地猜出了 eps，反解就能精确还原 x0</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    x0_hat </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (xt </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.sqrt(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ab) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> eps) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.sqrt(ab)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">    print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"          反解误差 = </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">np.abs(x0_hat </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> x0).max()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.2e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">          f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"   误差放大系数 = </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">np.sqrt(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ab) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.sqrt(ab)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.2f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">x"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span></code></pre>36:["$","figure",null,{"className":"my-5","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-line bg-surface","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line bg-surface-2 px-3.5 py-1.5","children":["$","span",null,{"className":"font-mono text-[11px] text-muted","children":"forward_diffusion.py"}]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"vml-code overflow-x-auto px-1 py-3 text-[12.5px] leading-[1.7]","dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$95"}}]]}],"$L96"]}]
97:T47ca,<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line" data-line="1"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="2"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.nn </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> nn</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="3"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="4"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">T </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1000</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="5"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">device </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "cuda"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.cuda.is_available() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "cpu"</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="6"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">betas </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.linspace(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1e-4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0.02</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, T, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">device</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">device)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="7"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">alphas </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> betas</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="8"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">alpha_bars </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.cumprod(alphas, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">dim</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="9"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="10"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="11"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> UNet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">nn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Module</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="12"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """这里只给接口。真实实现 = 下采样/上采样的卷积块 + 每层的自注意力 +</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="13"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    把整数 t 编成向量的时间嵌入（正弦位置编码后接两层 MLP），</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="14"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    带文本条件时再在每个块里插一层 cross-attention。</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="15"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    签名必须是 (x_t, t) -> 与 x_t 同形状的 eps 预测。"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="16"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> forward</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(self, x_t: torch.Tensor, t: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="17"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        raise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> NotImplementedError</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="18"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="19"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="20"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">model </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> UNet().to(device)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="21"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">opt </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">lr</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2e-4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="22"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="23"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> x0 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> dataloader:                                  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># x0: (B, C, H, W)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    x0 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> x0.to(device)                                 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 调度表在 device 上，数据也得过去</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    B </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> x0.shape[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    t </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.randint(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, T, (B,), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">device</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">device)       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 每条样本抽一个 t</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    eps </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.randn_like(x0)                         </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 这就是标签</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    ab </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> alpha_bars[t].view(B, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)                </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 索引张量必须和被索引的表同设备</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="29"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="30" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    x_t </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ab.sqrt() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> x0 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ab).sqrt() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> eps       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 重参数化，一步到位</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="31" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    eps_hat </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> model(x_t, t)                            </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 网络只做一件事：猜 eps</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="32" data-hl="true"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    loss </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.nn.functional.mse_loss(eps_hat, eps)  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 普通 MSE，没有别的项</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="33"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="34"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    opt.zero_grad()</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="35"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    loss.backward()</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="36"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    opt.step()</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="37"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="38"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="39"><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">@torch.no_grad</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">()</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="40"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> sample</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(shape):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="41"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """DDPM 反向采样，对应课文里的第四个公式。"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="42"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    x </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.randn(shape, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">device</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">device)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="43"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> t </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> reversed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">range</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(T)):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="44"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        t_batch </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.full((shape[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">],), t, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">device</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">device, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">dtype</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">torch.long)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="45"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        eps_hat </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> model(x, t_batch)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="46"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="47"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        ab </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> alpha_bars[t]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="48"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">        mean </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (x </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> betas[t] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ab).sqrt() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> eps_hat) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> alphas[t].sqrt()</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="49"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> t </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="50"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">            ab_prev </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> alpha_bars[t </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="51"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">            var </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> betas[t] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ab_prev) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ab)   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># beta 波浪</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="52"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">            x </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> mean </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> var.sqrt() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> torch.randn_like(x)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="53"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="54"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">            x </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> mean</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="55"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> x</span></span></code></pre>37:["$","figure",null,{"className":"my-5","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-line bg-surface","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line bg-surface-2 px-3.5 py-1.5","children":["$","span",null,{"className":"font-mono text-[11px] text-muted","children":"ddpm_minimal.py"}]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"vml-code overflow-x-auto px-1 py-3 text-[12.5px] leading-[1.7]","dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$97"}}]]}],"$L98"]}]
